- **epsl-cover-letter**: Use when writing the cover letter and editor-facing metadata for an Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) submission. It drafts the letter; it does not contact editors. - **epsl-data-analysis**: Use when reducing and reporting data for an Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) manuscript — full analytical-uncertainty budgets for isotope and geochronology data, defensible statistics (MSWD, weighted means, Bayesian age models), and inversion/model diagnostics. It guides reduction and reporting norms; it does not fabricate results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Earth-and-Planetary-Science-Letters-Skills/skills/epsl-data-analysis) · ★1.1k)
- **epsl-figures-and-tables**: Use when building figures and tables for an Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) manuscript. It designs exhibits; it does not run the analysis. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Earth-and-Planetary-Science-Letters-Skills/skills/epsl-figures-and-tables) · ★1.1k)
